### E-087 · B-030 (U7 step 1): the family manifest as the section contract, with motion tiers and a shared fallback (s42)
- **State before (the work tree after freeze 1):** four family layouts (`components/invitation/families/*.tsx`), each one component with its section order written inline; `FAMILIES[id].motionLevel` (0 / 1) checked against the presence of `inv-rise` in the layout's source; no manifest, no order rules, no fallback for a section a family does not draw (§8 of `08-template-system-and-harness.md` specified them).
- **Acceptance (brief and §8):** a manifest per family (sections and order rules, event types, photo slots, logo frames, motion tier, music and cover gate, palettes, byte budget) validated by the harness; a missing section degrades to the shared block, never a blank; at least three distinct compositions through the same contract, one non-wedding; existing rendering and the recorded default for new invitations preserved; legacy fixtures, 360 / 390 / 412 / wide, stress content, contrast, print, reduced motion, provenance and Q-D17 budgets verified; photo-heavy totals outside gate coverage identified.
- **Change (s42):**
  - `lib/invitation/family-manifest.ts`: `SECTION_IDS` (16 sections, also the canonical order), `CONTRACT_ORDER_RULES` (events before the RSVP and the gift, the gift before the RSVP, the RSVP before the guestbook), `MOTION_TIERS` (0 still, 1 entrance `inv-rise`; no higher tier until it has a budget, a reduced-motion fallback and a review) with `motionClasses`, `FamilyManifest`, `FAMILY_MANIFESTS` for the four families, `composedSectionOrder` (declared sections plus any undeclared one at its canonical place, minus what the cover draws) and `validateFamilyManifest`. The compositions: Gapura and Kartu ceremonial (greeting → quote → profiles → events → ceremony → programme …); Riang event-led (profiles → events → ceremony → programme → greeting → quote …); Lontar programme-led (profiles → ceremony → programme → events …, `coverIncludes: ['greeting']`). `startsNewFor` matches `defaultFamilyFor` (F-02 unchanged).
  - `components/invitation/family-sections.tsx`: `composeSections(manifest, renderers, fallback)` calls each renderer once in the manifest's order; `sharedSections(props, view)` draws every content section from the shared blocks on the palette tokens (the fallback).
  - The four layouts draw their sections as renderers keyed by `SectionId` and return `composeSections(FAMILY_MANIFESTS.<id>, …)`; each section root carries `data-section="<id>"`; Gapura's and Riang's entrance classes come from `motionClasses(manifest.motionTier).rise(…)` (Riang's alternating bands count in render order). `FAMILIES[].motionLevel` is removed (the manifest is the one source). Legacy is untouched.
  - `pnpm harness families` (`tools/template-harness/families.ts`) validates every manifest and its budget against `PRODUCT_PAGE_BUDGETS` / `FAMILY_BUDGET_BASIS`; the harness skill's family contract names the manifest, `composeSections` and `motionClasses`.
  - Tests: `tests/b030-family-manifest.test.ts` (12) — valid manifests and the validator's refusals; every event type and the F-02 starts; canonical placement; **every review page (212 family renders) in its manifest order under the contract rules**; three compositions on the same wedding content and on non-wedding pages (Riang birthday, Lontar corporate programme; the same programme on Gapura keeps its order); a changed manifest order or tier changes the page and nothing else; the shared fallback draws an undeclared quote and streaming section (and the shared forms) and nothing without content; motion (only `inv-rise`, never on names or controls, the only `@keyframes`); photo slots, portrait and logo frames against the layout code; provenance (CSS-only ornament, the briefs, two fonts), music (no layout `<audio>` or gate), palettes and budget; the markers are the only added markup. `tests/design-families.test.ts` reads the tier from the manifest and refuses a hard-coded `inv-rise`; three tests that pin pre-B-030 output (`b019-image-variants`, `b023-couple-profiles`, `s24-phone-covers`) compare the page without the markers (`withoutSectionMarkers`) or read the header's class wherever it sits.
- **Evidence (s42, the work tree after freeze 1):**
  - **Rendering preserved:** 780 renders (290 review fixtures, the seed's every option, × no guest and two stress guests, fixed clock) taken before the change are byte-identical after it once ` data-section="…"` is removed (`/vercel/share/work/s42-b030/`, `snapshot.ts` + `compare.py`; the baseline rendered twice identically first); legacy pages carry no marker. No stylesheet or script selects `data-section`, so layout at every width, print, forced colours and reduced motion cannot change; the final-tree product gates re-measure them.
  - Typecheck 0; unit **733 / 733** before the manifest-drives test was added, B-030 file **12 / 12** after; `pnpm harness families` PASS (4 manifests).
  - **Mutation check** (scratch `/vercel/share/work/s42-b030-mutation.qPdt`; the project untouched): composing in the canonical order instead of the manifest's fails 4 tests; a hard-coded `inv-rise` in Kartu fails 2; the validator without the contract rules fails 1; composing without the shared fallback fails 1.
- **Limits:** the 64 catalog registry entries still have no manifest (§7 step 1; with G-041 / G-062); the host cannot choose an order yet (G-041 will constrain it to the manifest). Photo-heavy pages (`review-<layout>-photos` with uploaded cover, couple and gallery photos; `review-<family>-gallery-<grid|masonry|carousel>`) are outside the byte gates: product-bytes measures the full, long-programme and music contents only, and the gallery gate checks layouts, lightbox and facades, not totals (Q-D17 photo-heavy evidence stays open).

## Session 43–44 (s43 G-016 / G-041 slices in the transfer `3bcc0c0`, tree `bf2a190`, 2,108 paths; finished, verified and accepted in s44, worksheet `sessions/session-44-20261110-1444.md`)

### E-088 · G-016 (U7): self-hosted, original theme art for every catalog template, and legible text over it (s43–s44)
- **State before (`3bcc0c0`):** 26 of 64 catalog templates (marble, gold, night) served from 36 self-hosted WebPs with a provenance register; 38 templates hotlinked Unsplash photos at runtime; no served-build request capture.
- **Acceptance (brief, backlog line G-016):** no runtime request to a third-party image endpoint on the catalog, previews and guest pages; every asset with a licence record; template IDs and invitation behaviour preserved; template art kept apart from invitation config, host uploads and `/media/`; rollback documented with the fallback boundary explicit.
- **Change (s44):** 56 originals generated from text prompts only (no input image, people's faces, text or logos; prompts verbatim), reviewed as contact sheets and as cut; 141 derivatives (cover 960, card 480, gallery 720 px wide, 4:5, WebP q78 m6; 11 stepped to q66–74 for their budget, recorded as `quality`); `theme-art-register.ts` +56 entries, the 14 s43 entries untouched; `theme-art.ts` documents the boundary: `REMOTE_THEME_ART_POOLS` = watercolor, lavender, graduation, which no catalog template uses. **R12 (legibility, found by the served-build measurement):** card and preview-gate labels at full strength and a centred plate in the palette's surface over the wash (`templates.module.css`, `catalog-ui.module.css`, `[slug]/preview.module.css`, `[slug]/art.module.css`).
- **Evidence (s44):** `tests/g016-theme-art.test.ts` 8 / 8 (files byte-identical to the register, licence records in the harness shape, own-pool resolution, the boundary, the rollback fingerprint, template-owned art, no other Unsplash builder, the R12 rules; mutation caught 7 / 8). **Served build `cn8YOStwlyXatpt_OdIiW`, 32 / 32:** curated (27) and all 64 cards on the register's card file, lazy and decorative; 11 art files before scrolling and 50 after, all 200 `image/webp`; zero third-party requests; CSP report-only with no img-src violation; 4:5 crops; overlay text 192 on 64 cards min 5.33:1 (110 below before R12); 19 pool previews request only their own set (preview gate 114 texts min 6.29:1; 29 below before); guest pages request no template art. ### E-091 · B-032 / I-13 (G-060) and I-11 (U7): the memorial occasion with sober defaults, the countdown switch, and religious text by approved corpus reference only (s47–s48; D-068)
- **State before (`434b753`):** the `tahlilan` category's preset (one catalog template today, `149 doa-khusyuk`) created a plain `tasyakuran` invitation: music and gift from the event type, the countdown always shown for a future published event, a preset opening sentence with religious wording (`Dengan memohon rahmat dan maghfirah Allah SWT…`, outside the corpus, ADR-O04), and the wizard, editor, profile heading ("Tentang Syukuran") and cover-gate kicker ("Undangan Tasyakuran") in thanksgiving words. The quote block rendered only host-typed text; nothing could reference the verified corpus.
- **Acceptance (s48 brief §2, backlog B-032 / I-13 / I-11):** new memorials carry `settings.occasion = memorial`, start on the calm family with F-02 on, with music, gift and countdown off; wizard, preview and editor use memorial labels and the right person fields, and typed names or trial state never cross incompatible occasions; a celebration on the same `tasyakuran` type and every stored invitation without an occasion behave exactly as before; the countdown switch persists an explicit boolean through save, reload, preview and guest render while calendar links stay; theme choices, profile-image access, RSVP and event ids keep their boundaries; the empty corpus renders no quotation, and approved, pending, withdrawn, unknown and cleared references behave, with provenance for eligible entries (fictional test data only).
- **Change:**
  - s47 (Carried from s47; code read and verified in s48): `lib/invitation/occasion.ts` (`OCCASIONS = ['memorial']`, `occasionOfCategory` maps `tahlilan`, `occasionOf` / `isMemorial` read only a known value, `MEMORIAL_COPY` neutral labels; no runtime imports so the client editor can use it). `detail-config.ts`: `settings.occasion` (enum), `settings.countdown` (boolean), `quote.corpusId` (`''` or a kebab id ≤ 120). `content-contract.ts`: the three fields `rendered`. `templates.ts`: the memorial preset `settings {language, occasion: memorial, countdown: false}`, `music` and `gift` off, the religious opening sentence removed. `trial.ts` and `app/buat/*`: memorial trial fields (one name, `Nama almarhum/almarhumah`), save issues checked against the occasion, a memorial first event `Tahlilan & Doa Bersama`. `families.ts`: `defaultFamilyFor(…, 'memorial')` → Gapura. `theme-switch.ts`: a memorial is offered memorial themes only. `ceremony-profile.ts`: the memorial profile (Nama lengkap, Keluarga, Pesan keluarga) under "Dalam Kenangan". `guest-view.ts`: `showCountdown` also needs `settings.countdown !== false`, and the calendar entry no longer depends on the countdown; `view.quote` resolves a reference through `verifiedBlockFor` (text, citation, `provenanceLine`) and never falls back to the typed text when a reference is set. The families (through `family-sections.tsx`) render `view.quote` with its provenance; `guest-blocks.tsx` shows the calendar entry without the countdown; `public-renderer.tsx` gives a memorial the `Undangan Tahlilan & Doa` gate kicker. `religious-text.ts`: `verifiedBlockFor`, `provenanceLine`, `approvedCorpusOptions`, and `findUnverifiedReligiousText` reports a reference that is not approved. Editor: memorial labels and profile form, the countdown switch (always a boolean), the corpus select (approved entries only; `''` clears). `tests/b032-memorial.test.ts`; `e047-child-photo-access` (the upload-field count) and `f02-default-family` adjusted.
  - s48: **R-S1** (script, not product) repaired in `verify-content-api.ts`; the B-032 / I-11 end-to-end checks themselves are new in s48 (six per writer in `verify-content-api.ts`, four per writer plus a cross-writer comparison in `verify-creation-api.ts`). **R-P1** (product, latent): the Quote tab's hint promised that an entry replaces the typed quote on every invitation, but the frozen legacy layout renders only `quote.text` (D-041); the hint now follows `familyOf(theme.familyId)`, the renderer's own test, and a new test pins both the legacy render and the hint.
- **Mechanism vs content (I-11):** the mechanism ships: a quote may name a corpus entry by id; the editor offers approved entries only; the design families print the entry's exact text, citation and a provenance line (`Sumber: <source>[, <translation>]. Diperiksa oleh <verifier> (<date>).`); a pending, withdrawn (no longer approved), unknown or cleared reference renders nothing, never the host's typed text in its place; the religious-text gate reports a reference that is not approved. **The corpus ships empty** (`content/verified-religious-texts/corpus.json`, 0 entries): no page shows a quotation from it and the editor offers none; entries need the owner's verbatim text, source, licence, verifier and approval. **D-041 legacy exception:** the legacy layout is frozen and keeps rendering only the stored `quote.text` and author, never an entry or its provenance; its editor hint says so.

After R-P1: typecheck exit 0, focused 34 / 34. Carried from s47: five mutations caught by the focused tests; stubbed-Clerk browser 51 / 51 (its harness and captures did not survive, so run 4 replaced it).

- **Findings:** **R-H4** (harness, s50 scratch only): the acceptance script's guest lookup filtered on `guests.deleted_at`, a column only invitations have; the query was corrected and the run repeated in full. **R-X2** (environment): the hosting platform had rewritten `.gitignore` to its scaffold (the documented sweep hazard, LATEST §6); restored from the research index before any record or commit.

Test `g029-map-facade` (9).

### E-095 · G-046 (U7): the owner's version history with paging, restore as one new version and undo (s50 code; finished s51)
- **State before (`0590814`):** the history page `/dashboard/invitations/[id]/history`, the restore route `POST /api/invitations/[id]/versions/[versionNo]/restore` and the facade `restoreInvitationVersion` had landed (s50), but the full suite failed one test (853 / 854) and served acceptance had not run.
- **Acceptance (s51 brief §3, backlog G-046):** restoring a version restores config only, never payments or guests; one new version under the normal version lock; stale, current, unknown, malformed, unrestorable, unauthorised and flag-off cases with zero writes on refusal; D-036 event identity, D-040 media references and B-041 entitlement on the restored config; non-config rows identical before and after; both writers agree; served acceptance of the editor-to-history entry, keyboard, confirm / cancel, stale tab, reload, preview and flag-off rollback.
- **Change (s51):** the single-writer pin in `tests/b035-content-writer.test.ts` now reads the `writeContent` dispatcher alone (no `try` / `catch`, the Rust branch never names `next.`, each writer called once) and proves that save and restore each dispatch once and never call a writer themselves, and that restore's only `catch` guards `prepareRestoreWrite` before any write (a test-scope defect, not a routing defect). **R-X4** (product): `loadVersionHistory` fetched `limit × 2 + 1` rows and dropped repeated numbers in JavaScript, so with enough pre-B-035 repeats older versions became unreachable; now `versionHistoryQuery` (`SELECT DISTINCT ON (version_no) … ORDER BY version_no DESC, id DESC LIMIT limit + 1`), the row a restore reads. **R-X5** (product): the editor's first screen carries the "Riwayat versi" link (it showed only inside a section). **R-X6** (product, accessibility): a page-level `HistoryAnnouncer` (`role="status"`, kept through `router.refresh()`, takes focus) announces a successful restore; refusals stay beside their button. `FLAG_VERSION_HISTORY` (default on); `BACKEND_CONTENT` default `next`.
- **Evidence (New in s51):** focused 20 / 20; full suite 855 / 855 (tree `6cd308b`); `verify-content-api` 94 / 94 through Next.js and the real Rust binary (per writer: unrestorable 422, another invitation's protected image 400 `INVALID_MEDIA`, a Premium palette on Standard 403 `THEME_NOT_ENTITLED`, each with zero writes; a repeated number restores its latest row as one new version keeping the event id; pages of two list every number once down to version 1; another customer gets none). Isolated mutation controls m1–m5 caught (m5 after widening the pin). **Browser 25 / 25** (build `sq__ZFoPRdwaBqXePO_xP`, database `s51_browser_g046c`, real proxy, cookie Clerk stub; :3401 with the flag off): 33 versions through the editor's real save; both links reached by Tab and Enter; 30 per page newest first, `?sebelum=4` = 3, 2, 1, each number once; changed sections named in the editor's words; axe clean, no overflow at 390 px; confirm / cancel by keyboard with focus kept; restore → version 34 announced, one live row, non-config rows unchanged; reload, editor, owner preview and public page read it; undo 33 → 35; a stale tab refused with nothing written; route refusals other 404, admin 403, signed out 401, malformed 400, unknown 404, current 409; flag off: no link, page and route 404, versions kept. Local checkpoint `9485cda` (tree `6cd308b`). Later trees keep it green (content 104 / 104 on the I-21 tree, including I-21's restore of a version from before the memory).
- **Exact tree:** F2 `a4fcf74` (tree `953fd62`; receipt in E-098); served run 5's G-046 section 27 / 27 on it (history paging, config-only restore, the stale-tab refusal, undo, flag off). s51 recorded a pre-freeze only (corrected s53).
- **Limits:** real-Clerk acceptance is DA-01 item 18; the browser ran through the default `next` writer (the Rust writer is covered end to end and by the cross-writer comparison).
- **Risk / rollback:** `FLAG_VERSION_HISTORY=off` hides the link, the page and the route (404); versions are kept. No migration.
